14.1 S1 facts (2026-11-04, verified in source): config keys `allow_signal` (`TargetConfig.AllowSignal`, per target), `commands.enabled` (`cfg.CommandsEnabled()`), `checks.activity.by_application` (`cfg.Checks["activity"].ByApplication`), database budget `targets[].databases.max` (default 10; reasons `db_budget`, `discovered_after_start`). `check_skip` reasons (`Requirements.Supports`, internal/check/check.go): `role <r> not allowed`, `requires PG >= <v>, got <v>`, `requires PG <= <v>, got <v>`, `requires <tier>, got <tier>`, `missing extension <ext>`. `setting` facts (internal/check/settings.go) carry label `source`; only the allowlist (includes `track_io_timing`) is always emitted, every other setting (`shared_preload_libraries`, `compute_query_id`, `track_activity_query_size`, `pg_stat_statements.track`) is emitted ONLY when it differs from `boot_val`, and connection-sourced values (`client`/`session`/`user`) are dropped. Consequence for the evaluator: with `SettingsKnown` (a `setting` fact or the `settings` check seen in the window) an absent non-allowlisted setting means its default (`shared_preload_libraries` empty, `compute_query_id` auto, `track_activity_query_size` 1024); without it the setting-based items are omitted. `SessionQueryText` has no agent setting yet → agent leaves it nil (RDY-SESS-01 never fires until one exists; recorded §8 D-RDY-1).

§8 | 1 | bug-DL observations (not defects, recorded) | — | (a) after the last deadlock the alert holds ≈ lookback 2 min + `metricGrace` 10 min (resolution measured 15:31:14 for a last positive sample at 15:19:24); a server restart restores the episode with a full new grace (`Evaluator.Restore`), so a deploy extends the hold; (b) the Locks "Deadlock rate" tile shows the newest per-database rate, so it returns to 0 ≈ 30–45 s after the last deadlock; (c) a real deadlock clears in `deadlock_timeout` (1 s) and the 10 s lock sample catches the A↔B cycle only sometimes (seen live, rendered with "Cycle detected"); (d) live checks also passed for temp files (Overview), idle in transaction (`conn.idle_in_transaction` fired after 300 s + 120 s, resolved on release), `txn.long_running` (fired at 900 s + 120 s, survived a restart, resolved on release), Google Chat deliveries ok; (e) the dev Docker VM disk is 88 % used (3.6 GB free): `disk.free_low` fires correctly; the Docker daemon is shared with other projects, so nothing was pruned | supervisor (coordinator) | — |
§8 | 1 | BUG-RET (found at P11 review) | D17 / 11.3 Retention API | a confirmed retention change removed and re-added the TimescaleDB policy; 2.31 runs a new job at once, so a shortening deleted chunks within seconds although the API, UI and README promise deletion at the policy's next run (live: job 1019 `metrics_ash` replaced 16:58:31, ran 16:58:32 — a lengthening, no data lost). Fix: an existing policy is changed in place (`alter_job(job_id, config => jsonb_set(config,'{drop_after}',…), if_exists => true)`: same id, schedule and `next_start`); a missing policy is added and its `next_start` moved one day ahead in the same transaction. RETINT003 fails on the old code | supervisor (R-P11, R-P11b) | — |
§8 | 1 | P11 Odoo-DB observations (not defects) | — | churn table `qa_tmp.churn` (200 000 rows, 100 000 dead, autovacuum off): pglens tables view shows dead 100 000 / ratio 0.5 at the next 5-min sample; advisor opens `table.dead_tuples_high`, `table.never_autovacuumed`, `table.autoanalyze_stale`, then `table.seq_scan_heavy` under a 7-min seq-scan load; statements view shows the probe query (11 322 calls vs 11 483 run, the rest after the last sample; mean 15 ms); after `VACUUM ANALYZE` all four resolve within one advisor cycle (~8 min). Seq-scan rate 80/s for 27 queries/s: each parallel scan counts leader + 2 workers (PostgreSQL behaviour). Gap: `vacuum.disabled` checks only the global `autovacuum`; a table with `autovacuum_enabled=false` is reported only indirectly (never vacuumed / dead tuples) | supervisor | — |
§8 | 1 | 11.5b deviations (R-11.5b) | Q2 design D1–D7 | (a) one `notify.Slot` (atomic pointer) per kind shared by the alert path and the test send; DB commit before swap; (b) every channel (env or console) uses a client that never follows redirects (3xx = failed, not retried); (c) the test-send 409 detail now names the console option; (d) startup read failure → env fallback, logged, not retried; (e) NOTI-E2E-002 in its own spec file `notification_secret.spec.ts` (top-level `test.use` trace/video/screenshot off; a describe-level `use` broke the whole `console_visual.spec.ts`), helpers in `web/e2e/notification_secrets.ts`, `fullHdRoute` exported from `fullhd_routes.ts`; (f) URL field is a CSS-masked text input outside a form (no password-manager prompt); (g) LIMITS §15: stored without encryption at rest, statement logging of the pglens DB can capture it, a console user can make the server POST to any https or private http endpoint; a second server replica sees a change only at restart | supervisor (R-11.5b) | — |
§8 | 1 | 12.1 deviations (R-12.1) | contract 12.1 / D20 | (a) `StatementsResponse.truncated` keeps its agent-side meaning; the filtered-set truth is the new optional `has_more` (sent only with `q`, `limit+1` row) — I-9; (b) empty `q=` = no search on statements/findings, 400 on `searchInstance` (required); (c) `searchInstance` also takes `database` and `limit` 1–50 (capped), 503 without a pool; (d) the total is `count(*) OVER ()` inside each kind's single query (one query per kind, same predicate); (e) snippets fold white space; a hit matched on another field shows the start of its text unmarked; (f) tables match on `schema.table` only — column names are the Columns kind (D20 text ambiguous); columns match on `schema.table.column` or type; (g) the statement link carries `from`/`to` (UTC RFC 3339) only when the caller passed them; `/queries` refuses ranges > 30 days, the server does not; (h) `focus` and plain `q` are read only once 12.2 lands (12.2 contract covers both; Slowest queries currently reads `slow.q`); Index analysis shows current metrics only, so 12.2 needs a "not in the current list" state | R-12.1 | 12.2 |
§8 | 1 | 13.3 deviations (R-13.3, R-13.3b, live) | contract 13.3 (banners above `<Outlet/>`) | (a) from 1600 px `InstanceStatusBanners` renders in the shell `Header` row (portal node, one live region, one request; zero added height), below 1600 px above `<Outlet/>` — the contract placement pushed `/instances/:id/queries` to 996 px at 1920×950 (I-13); (b) a failing check keeps the banner ("N of M checks have a current result", failing check named) — truthful, documented in `instance.coverage`; (c) `indexCleanup` takes the configured window from `useAdvisorWindows` when no open finding carries it; `CleanupPanel` touched; (d) `UNUSED_INDEX_MIN_BYTES` (10 MiB) mirrors `unusedIndexBytes` in the UI; (e) `/locks` is `pendingUnit: 16.3` → COV-E2E-001 records UNVERIFIED there (banner visible); (f) the 1600 px header checks are written in the spec with the `assertFits` scroll measurement (assertFits measures 1920 only); (g) COV-E2E-001 must run alone right after an agent restart (a combined run started it 124 s late) | R-13.3, R-13.3b, live | 14.2 |
§8 | 1 | 12.2 deviations (R-12.2, R-12.2b) | contract 12.2 | (a) the hint "Searches everything pglens has collected in this range" is visible below 1600 px only; from 1600 px sr-only + tooltip (D26); (b) empty-state actions are named "Show all statements" / "Show all findings" / "Search all states" / "Clear filters" / "Clear the search" (no duplicate "Clear search" name); (c) `useFindings` gains `keepPrevious`; labels/marks follow the last successful term (`useShownTerm`); (d) the Index analysis search-results state is a user-action state outside the Full HD route list, lists capped at 160 px from 1600 px (`fullhd-inventory.md` "Search states"), 12.3 measures it live; (e) SearchEverything resolves a relative range once per term and passes the page database; hit links take the page range keys; (f) tests removed/adapted: SLOWLIST-005 and the toolbar-search part of TBL-UI-027 (the single box replaces the toolbar search, D20), FHD-APPLY-015 locates the description span via `selector`; `slow.q` and `statement-statistics.q` migrate to `q` on load | R-12.2, R-12.2b | 12.3 |
§8 | 1 | P13 closure changes (R-P13, R-P13b) | D22b as implemented at 13.2 | (a) `peak_scan_rate` and standby scans (`last_idx_scan` label, metrics) are bounded by the unused-index window; `history_days` keeps the full history; (b) D22b shortcut implemented: PG16+ index with `last_idx_scan` older than the window and no scan pglens saw inside it is judged at once (`observed_source` stays `postgres_stats`, evidence `last_scan`) — closes the 13.2 follow-up; (c) a scan pglens saw inside the window with short history now gives no finding (was degraded); (d) small/PK/unique indexes never get a degraded `index.unused`; (e) the engine reads the `metrics_indexes` retention once per pass: window > retention → degraded reason names it; (f) `backup.no_strategy` degrades only when no base backup was ever seen, detail says "in N days of monitoring"; (g) INFO (R-P13 #9): a schedule rebuild reruns every check once — accepted, no change; (h) ServerInfo static limits text already accurate | R-P13, R-P13b | — |
§8 | 1 | bug-HDR, 12.3 and P12 notes | contract 12.3 / I-13 | (a) bug-HDR found by the coordinator on a live screenshot (pre-existing from 82f4650); from 1600 px to 1919 px Index analysis headers may wrap (85 px Indexes views, 73 px Dead rows) — 1600 is not a reference viewport (overview A2), recorded in `fullhd-inventory.md`; (b) view titles shortened ("Index findings", "Unindexed foreign keys"), Findings link compact from 1600 px (name stays "Open Findings"); (c) HDR-E2E-001 lives in new `web/e2e/index_header.spec.ts`; (d) README search docs were committed with unit:P13:1 (shared file); (e) OpenAPI drops raw `minLength`/`maxLength` on search terms (server trims, then counts code points); (f) G-UI-LEGACY combined run lost `TestUI_Failover` to a shared Playwright results dir (ENOENT on traces) — do not run live Playwright while the legacy UI e2e runs | coordinator, R-bug-HDR, R-12.3, R-P12 | — |
§8 | 1 | Q2 resolved (user, 2026-10-30: "si, salva") | overview Q2, settings-inventory §5 | pglens stores webhook URLs write-only in its database, no encryption at rest in v1 (documented), env stays the fallback. 11.5b unblocked; supervisor design D1–D7 for 11.5b (table `notification_channels`, `PUT`/`DELETE /api/v1/notification-channels/{kind}` operationIds `setNotificationChannel`/`deleteNotificationChannel`, validation https or http on loopback/private hosts ≤ 2048, DB overrides env, swappable channel holders so the alert path uses a change without restart, redaction of every URL ever configured, logs host only, write-only UI form + remove, NOTI-006..012, NOTI-INT-001, NOTI-UI-005..008, NOTI-E2E-002). Executed next, before 13.3 | user / supervisor | 11.5b |
§8 | 1 | 14.1 deviations (R-14.1, Sonnet + Opus advisor) | contract 14.1 | (a) absent non-allowlisted setting = its default only when `SettingsKnown`, otherwise the item is omitted; nil flags/role membership omit items; unknown tier = -1. (b) the DB budget is derived server-side: monitored count when ≥1 database has skip_reason `db_budget`, discovered = monitored + over-budget, else budget 0 (unknown). (c) `HostSampleWindow` exported from `internal/readiness`. (d) `standbySteps`: on a standby every SQL fix except `ALTER SYSTEM` starts with a step to run it on the primary. (e) RDY-SKIP-001 (`internal/check/skip_reason_test.go`) pins the shapes of `Requirements.Supports` reasons the evaluator parses. (f) `agent_flags.by_application` reports the effective value of the activity check (`SetByApplication`/`ByApplicationEnabled`), not the YAML key. (g) `session_query_text` is nil (unknown) until 16.1 wires an agent setting; 16.1 must emit it (D-RDY-1). (h) a config change needs an agent restart to reach agent_flags (hourly result). (i) Replace-fact prune is `last_seen < result.TS`: equal timestamps do not prune; RDY-INT-001 pushes the second result at +1 s. (j) `postgres:18.6-alpine` and `postgres:15.19-alpine` ARE local (used for SQL/G-RO checks); the TimescaleDB image is not. (k) environment: Go module downloads (build/test, testcontainers deps) occurred (REQ-24); commits use author `Fabio <fabio@git.it>` via `git -c` per the user; tgsend at `/home/agent/.bin/tgsend`. (l) roster: Sonnet 5.5 implements every unit by user authorisation, Opus 5.5 advisor. | advisor | 14.2 (consumes readiness API), 16.1 |
§8 | 1 | 14.2 deviations (Sonnet + Opus advisor) | contract 14.2 | (a) `ReadinessSection` expands a warning item by default only when it is the single warning (others collapsed) so the tab fits Full HD (I-13); (b) `ReadinessBadge` has `role=status` with aria-label from `readinessBadgeLabel`; banner link has aria-label `<text>. Open the readiness tab.`; (c) cluster-level badge aggregates the instance summary rows via `clusterReadiness`; (d) captions `instance.readiness`, `fleet.readiness`; (e) test adaptations: `FleetPage.test.tsx` beforeEach `getReadinessSummary` handler, `render.test.tsx`, `instanceSections.test.ts` (+`readiness` tab), `shell.test.tsx` UI-SHELL-011, `InstanceStatusBanners.test.tsx` beforeEach `getReadiness` full + INSTBAN-004; (f) docs: `docs/MONITORING_READINESS.md` "In the console"; (g) VIS-RDY-001 live spec appended, never executed | advisor | 14.3 |
§8 | 1 | 16.1 deviations (Sonnet + Opus advisor) | contract 16.1 test-expectation list | (a) the two replaced tests and two new ExplicitFalse tests as contracted. (b) beyond the list, accepted as supervisor decisions, no assertion weakened: `locks_test.go` row fixtures widened by 6 columns (mock scan needs the exact column count); `agent_flags_test.go` RDY-AGENT-001 `ByApplication` literals → `boolPtr`, and `SessionQueryText` `Nil` → `NotNil`+`False` (S5 now publishes it). §8 | 1 | 16.2 deviations (Sonnet + Opus advisor) | contract 16.2 | (a) the stored tree lives in `lock_snapshots` (one row per instance, PK tenant+instance), not a `lock_tree` fact: SESSAPI-INT-001 seeds `lock_snapshots` + `object_facts`. (b) `connections` is optional (absent when no tree ever sampled). (c) raw text is kept in `query` and mirrored in `query_text` (existing names unchanged). (d) normalised text that may hold a credential is replaced by `redact.Withheld` (extra safeguard). (e) summary keys: empty user/application/database = `""`, NULL client = `local`, NULL state = `unknown`. (f) extra tests SESSAPI-009/010/011; README `GET /api/v1/locks` section edited. (g) advisor finding fixed by supervisor: blocking-tree `nodes` also get server-set `origin` (SESSAPI-012), the badge of 16.3 needs it and D27 forbids UI re-derivation. | supervisor decision | 16.3 reads `origin` from sessions and nodes |
§8 | 1 | 16.3 deviations (Sonnet + Opus advisor) | contract 16.3 | (a) UI-LOCK-021 changed as contracted (ID kept, fixture `connections.by_application_enabled:false`) + SORIGIN-UI-011; no other existing test edited. (b) `SimpleTable` instead of `DataTable` (no row detail). (c) page tabs Blocking (default) / Application sessions / pglens sessions / Activity read from `?view=` (`locksViews.ts`) because the page reached 2776 px; fallback page unchanged; accepted: Blocking is first, not Application sessions. (d) `ActivitySection` props `connections`, `part`; 4 captions `locks.sessions-application`, `locks.sessions-pglens`, `locks.connection-summary`, `locks.origin`. (e) callout shown only when `by_application_enabled === false`; a server older than 16.2 shows none. ## 2026-11-05 P4 DONE (attempt 1); phase 4 compaction

Settled 4.1-4.5 evidence lines (§7), the 4.5 checkpoint (§6) and the P16 gate lines moved here. Board 4.1-4.5 stay DONE (rows unchanged); §8 R-P4 documents the closure fixes (table_indexes, not_table, index_unparsed, truncated inventory, unique-key coverage).

4.5 S1 (2026-11-05): ground-truth column `res_partner.email` (varchar, 30006 rows, 30002 distinct, no index on it or any expression of it; `res_partner__name_index` is the indexed control). `pg_stat_statements_reset()` run on the QA Odoo db before the load. Load: `/home/agent/pglens-odoo-stack/load/suggest_ground_truth.py` (PID file `/tmp/suggest_ground_truth.pid`, 330 s at 3.3/s, log `<scratchpad>/gt-load.log`). Stack redeployed (`deploy.sh`) with build of 214a981.
- 4.5 (2026-11-05; WIP b744fb6): ground truth load `suggest_ground_truth.py` 330 s, 1089 executions (3.3/s), `pg_stat_statements` after: calls 1089, total_exec_time 6036.4 ms (5.54 ms/call). G-DEPLOY (`deploy.sh`, images of this phase's build) 4 times (initial; after the reltuples fix; two density fixes), healthy each time. First VIS-040 runs found two defects, both fixed (not the assertions): (1) the restored stack has `n_live_tup` 0 for every table (`metrics_tables`), so the small-table rule (`LiveTuples < 1000`) skipped `res_partner` (30006 rows, reltuples 30006) and 20 other tables: the rule now reads `reltuples` first and `n_live_tup` only when reltuples is 0 (as size and selectivity already did), IDXS-010 adapted, IDXS-029 added; (2) the suggestions page overflowed 1920×950 by 17 px: coverage line `min-[1600px]:py-0 min-[1600px]:text-xs` (page 951 px with py-0.5, 950 with py-0). Result: `GET …/index-suggestions?window=24h&database=odoo` lists `res_partner(email)` (Strong, weight 6.5 %, est. size ~1.0 MiB, create statement `CREATE INDEX CONCURRENTLY idx_res_partner_email ON public.res_partner (email);`), no suggestion leads with `res_partner.name` (indexed control); coverage 47 of 140 statements analysed. VIS-040 `-g VIS-040` 1 passed (7.6 s; screenshots `test/e2e/_artifacts/ui/visual/vis-040-suggestions-{1920,1440}.png`, assertFits both Full HD viewports); QA-CLEAN-001 passed after it; vitest SuggestionsPanel 14 passed, lint 0 errors, prettier clean. Ground-truth comparison: the 24 h window averages the statement over every scrape in the window (R-4.3a), so the displayed read cost (0.094 ms/s) is 6036 ms spread over about 64 000 s of scrapes, not the 18 ms/s of the 5.5-minute burst; size ~1.0 MiB vs formula 30006 × (avg width + 12) × 1.15. C-4 review (advisor, Opus 5.5) found two defects, fixed (final build deployed a 5th time, VIS-040 1 passed in 8.2 s, QA-CLEAN-001 passed): (1) the coverage line did not add up (statements carry several skip reasons, and a statement whose only predicate group was a small table counted as analysed): `statements_analysed` now counts statements with at least one candidate, `skipped` keeps one count per reason and is documented as overlapping (OpenAPI, README, comment); the line reads "Analysed N of M statements. Skipped (a statement can have several reasons): …" (IDXS-030, SUGG-LIB-006 with overlapping sum); (2) rows with no writes or unknown writes were "Write-heavy" because a tiny call rate over the 0.001 write floor gave ratio < 1: a table with no index writes (`noIndexWrites`: unknown, or Ins + (Upd − HotUpd) + Del < 0.001/s) is never write_heavy, floor moderate (IDXS-031); the UI shows `<0.01/s` and `<0.001 ms/s` for non-zero tiny values (SUGG-LIB-010). Reset side effect: no `metrics_statements` row with a negative rate between 10:15 and 10:21Z (0 rows) → reset handled. Evidence: `metrics_statements` 24 h window spans min ts 2026-11-04 17:10:20Z, 899 distinct scrapes; the final panel shows `res_partner(email)` Strong, weight 6.4 %, calls 0.02/s (1089 calls / ≈ 63 000 s), read/write ratio 16.5, read cost 0.092 ms/s (6036.4 ms / ≈ 65 000 s), ~1.0 MiB, writes `<0.01/s` ×3, labelled estimate and upper bound. 1440 px screenshot checked by eye: table and the statement code block scroll horizontally inside their card below 1600 px (expected: the Full HD rule starts at 1600 px), nothing overlaps. The coverage line is `truncate` with a `title` from 1600 px (it cannot wrap; fit margin 0 px at 1920×950 no longer depends on its length). VIS-040 searches `res_partner` first (page 1 only otherwise). IDXEINT re-run after the changes: ok. vitest `src/features/indexes src/lib src/api src/test src/routes` 77 files / 687 passed; golangci-lint 0 issues; G-API no diff after staging.

G-API: `make web-gen-api api-docs` no diff after commit, `contract.test.ts` 132 passed (fixture `getIndexSuggestions.ts`), `vitest run src/test src/api` 217 passed (one earlier run showed 4 failures, not reproduced in two reruns, cause not determined: load), README paragraph and route list. EXPLAIN (ANALYZE) on 100 k `metrics_statements` rows, test container: statements query 121 ms single instance; with a second instance holding 275 k rows the first plan (separate `count(DISTINCT ts)` CTE) skip-scanned `metrics_statements_ts_idx` and filtered the foreign rows (190 ms of 280 ms, growing with foreign data), so the scrape count now comes from the same scan via `GROUPING SETS ((datname, queryid), (ts))` over `metrics_statements_dedup_idx` (instance_id, ts range): 179 ms, independent of foreign rows; index_def/table_columns queries seq-scan `object_facts` (kind filter, sub-millisecond), tables query 0.13 ms. Review (advisor, Opus 5.5): no blocker; asked for (1) null-free raw JSON checks (added `requireNoNulls` to IDXE-003, IDXE-004 golden and every integration response: arrays and `skipped` never null), (2) multi-instance EXPLAIN (done, query changed), (3) web meta-tests (done), (4) query-text exposure same as search/statements (no redaction layer exists there, raw `query_text` returned alike).
- 4.4 (2026-11-05; WIP 4694e36): G-WEB-UNIT `TZ=UTC pnpm exec vitest run src/features/indexes src/lib src/routes src/api src/test` 77 files / 684 tests passed (SUGG-LIB-001..009, SUGG-UI-001..012 incl. axe); `pnpm run typecheck` clean; `pnpm run lint` 0 errors (2 pre-existing warnings); prettier clean. - UNBLOCKED 2026-11-04 by user ruling (rebuild stack, §8): was BLOCKED (14.2): live gates G-DEPLOY, VIS-RDY-001, G-QA need the Odoo stack, backups and `/home/agent/pglens-odoo-stack/.env` (webhook secret), all absent. Rebuilding the stack (phase 0.2) is the user's decision: needs secret and backups only the user holds. 14.3 also needs the stack; strict order stops the run here.
- RESOLVED 2026-11-06 (P17 closure): the unexplained `TestUI_FindingMute` failure of the second P15 G-UI-LEGACY run did not recur; passed in the P15 final runs and in the P17 full run (666 s, exit 0). Earlier root cause (16.4: `openFindingCard` clicked a row already expanded) fixed.
- No other blocker.

## 10. Do-not-repeat

- The grep hook denies symbol-like patterns in code files: use `tokensave_search` or prefix `TOKENSAVE_DISABLE_GREP_HOOK=1` for genuinely non-symbol greps.
- `tokensave_read` with `mode: "lines"` needs `lines: "a-b"`.
- The tokensave index serves `main` (branch `feat/index-analysis` is not tracked): read files changed on this branch directly; do not trust tokensave for them.
- Never put the webhook URL in any file or output.
- Git identity is not configured globally: commit with `git -c user.name=fabio -c user.email=fabio@git.it commit …` (the repository's existing author).
- Vitest keeps a module cache in `web/node_modules/.vitest-cache`: a new fixture file under `web/src/test/fixtures/` may not be seen by `contract.test.ts` until run with `--experimental.fsModuleCache=false` (10.1). Confirmed 14.2: stale cache ran 92 of 127 contract tests (16 fixtures skipped silently, incl. getReadiness*); moving `.vitest-cache` aside gave 127 and full suite 1546. Compare test counts after adding fixtures.
- `make build-images` (`--pull`) reached the registry once in 14.2 with user network authorisation (2026-11-04); images `ghcr.io/manprint/pglens-{agent,server}:dev` are now local (legacy UI e2e needs them).
- The background `deploy.sh` log goes to the scratchpad (`deploy<n>.log`).
- Never use `docker build --pull` / `make build-images` (reaches a registry): use `deploy.sh`.
- Never run `test/harness/dump_test.go` (`-tags=e2e`, image `timescale/timescaledb:2.29.0-pg17` is not local): `make vet-tags` only compiles it.
- Never `git add -A` and never discard `internal/webui/dist/index.html`: commit with explicit pathspecs and check `git diff --cached --name-only`.
- Never run two Playwright jobs (or a `make web-build`/e2e gate) at the same time against `test/e2e/_artifacts/ui/results`: traces collide (`ENOENT … recording.trace` on `context.close()`).
- After a stack restart check `docker ps -a` for a `Created` agent (`up -d --no-recreate pglens-agent`); an outage stays in every chart window it covers (6 h/24 h live ratios fail until it ages out).
- Units that change `api/openapi.yaml`: stage regenerated `generated.ts`/`docs/api.md` before G-API/G-WEB-STATIC.
- Server integration tests (`getSharedPool`) need `timescale/timescaledb:2.31.0-pg17`: absent here; verify SQL on `postgres:18.6-alpine` with minimal DDL (temporary test, delete after) and seed Replace facts with `wire.AgentFlagsFact`/`wire.AgentFlagsKey`, never a literal key.
